- **confusion-matrix-accuracy-auc-evaluation**: Use when comparing two or more supervised classifiers on the same held-out test split and you need comparable metrics (confusion matrix, accuracy, AUC) to rank them, e.g. malignant vs nonMalignant cell prediction. - **confusion-matrix-accuracy-benchmarking**: Use when predicted cell type labels from marker-based scoring (e.g., GeneVector phenotype_probability) must be validated against reference annotations via a confusion matrix with per-type accuracy and overall misclassification rate. - **confusion-matrix-accuracy-computation**: Use when evaluating a cell-type annotation method against known true labels: you have per-cell predicted labels and ground-truth labels for query datasets (e.g. GSE115746, GSE92332, GSE84133, GSE132044) and need accuracy and labeled accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/confusion-matrix-accuracy-computation))
- **confusion-matrix-accuracy-computation**: Use when classifying EMP samples into EMPO level 2/3 environment categories from Deblur community composition and you must report cross-validated confusion matrices with overall accuracy (target ~91% and ~84%). - **confusion-matrix-analysis**: Use when evaluating multiclass agreement between predicted ABCDEF protocol category assignments (e.g., from MedPAO's categorize_concepts tool or LLM baselines) and radiologist ground truth on MIMIC-CXR samples, where macro/weighted F1 and Jaccard are the reporting targets. - **confusion-matrix-analysis**: Use when evaluating classification performance of the DenseNet121 ensemble on raw LC-MS (.npy/.mzML) test samples — especially when comparing full-data vs biomarker-filtered (PC&gt;1/3/8, 3 ppm) models via Fig. 4f-style ablation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/confusion-matrix-analysis))
- **confusion-matrix-and-auc-visualization**: Use when evaluating a trained DeepMSProfiler (DenseNet121 ensemble) classifier on a held-out LC-MS test split and you need ROC/AUC curves and a per-class confusion matrix to confirm accuracy, precision, recall, F1, and AUC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/confusion-matrix-and-auc-visualization))
- **confusion-matrix-auc-benchmark-evaluation**: Use when semi-synthetic multi-omics data contain a known enriched target pathway and you need a quantitative detection benchmark (confusion-matrix AUC) over pathway importance statistics across effect sizes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/confusion-matrix-auc-benchmark-evaluation))
- **confusion-matrix-construction-single-cell**: Use when you have hybrid clustering assignments from ClonoCluster (over alpha = seq(0, 1, by = 0.1), beta = 0.1, res = 1) plus a barcode table, and need to quantify how well clusters recapitulate clonal origin. - **confusion-matrix-evaluation**: Use when comparing automated peak-filtering calls to manual peak-quality labels (e.g. Good/Bad Peak clicks) on LC-MS standard library peaks to quantify filtering precision, recall, and F1. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/confusion-matrix-evaluation))
- **confusion-matrix-evaluation-against-source-labels**: Use when predicted single-cell clone labels (e.g., from vireoSNP on mtDNA variants) must be validated against ground-truth source/population labels, and a concordance percentage or confusion matrix is the required benchmark output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/confusion-matrix-evaluation-against-source-labels))
- **confusion-matrix-evaluation-of-diagnostic-accuracy**: Use when an end-to-end LC-MS metabolomics classifier must be validated on held-out testing samples and its per-class accuracy visualized as a confusion matrix. - **confusion-matrix-generation**: Use when after training or evaluating a classification model (e.g., MS2DeepScore or other neural networks) when you have paired arrays of predicted class labels and ground-truth labels and need to assess per-class prediction accuracy, false positive/negative rates, or class imbalance effects. - **congestion-aware-axonal-delay-snn**: Congestion-Aware Dynamic Axonal Delay for Spiking Neural Networks. Activation: congestion-aware delay, dynamic axonal delay SNN, input-dependent delay, SNN temporal processing, adaptive delay learning. ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/neuroscience/congestion-aware-axonal-delay-snn) · ★4 · 3 copies)
- **congestion-aware-delay-snn**: Congestion-Aware Dynamic Axonal Delay mechanism for Spiking Neural Networks. Decomposes delay into channel-wise static base delay + global activity-conditioned shift. Reduces delay parameters by ~50% while improving accuracy on temporal tasks. Source: arXiv:2605.01291 (Bai et al., May 2026). ([hiyenwong/ai_collection](https://github.com/hiyenwong/ai_collection/tree/HEAD/collection/skills/neuroscience/congestion-aware-delay-snn) · ★4 · 3 copies)

([wiseppc/claude-code-plugin](https://github.com/wiseppc/claude-code-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/wiseppc/skills/connect))
- **connect**: Connect this agent to a person's Marfa instance through its MCP server, then confirm the connection by reading the instance overview. Use when someone wants to connect, add or set up Marfa, gives a Marfa instance address, or asks why the Marfa tools are missing. ([withmarfa/marfa-plugin](https://github.com/withmarfa/marfa-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/connect))
- **connect**: Use when the person asks to install, set up, sign in to, reconnect, or re-authorise Native in Claude Code, Codex, or another client; when Native's MCP tools are missing, disconnected, or report expired or missing OAuth; or when adding Native to a second client. Do not use for ordinary Native work once connected (use `enter`), for React Native, or for unrelated meanings of native. ([withnative/native-plugin](https://github.com/withnative/native-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/native/skills/connect))
- **connect**: Connect this Cursor to the person's Wybe AI colleague so that hooks feed it redacted session summaries and `remember` can store work facts. Triggers on a pasted sentence like "Koble meg til KI-kollegaen min i Wybe: https://kollega.wybe.me", "Koble til Wybe", "Connect me to my Wybe AI colleague at https://kollega.wybe.me", "connect to Wybe", or any request to pair Cursor with a Wybe address. ([wybe-labs/wybe-connect](https://github.com/wybe-labs/wybe-connect/tree/HEAD/packages/cursor/skills/connect))
- **connect**: Connect this Codex to the person's Wybe AI colleague so that a background distil feeds it the redacted essence of their work and `remember` can store work facts. Triggers on a pasted sentence like "Koble meg til KI-kollegaen min i Wybe: https://kollega.wybe.me", "Koble til Wybe", "Connect me to my Wybe AI colleague at https://kollega.wybe.me", "connect to Wybe", or any request to pair Codex with a Wybe address. ([wybe-labs/wybe-connect](https://github.com/wybe-labs/wybe-connect/tree/HEAD/packages/codex/skills/connect))
- **connect**: Connect this Claude Code to the person's Wybe colleague (their Wybe node) so that a background distil feeds it the redacted essence of their work and `remember` can store work facts. Triggers on a pasted sentence like "Koble meg til KI-kollegaen min i Wybe: https://kollega.wybe.me", "Koble meg til Wybe-kollegaen min: https://&lt;slug&gt;.wybe.me", "Koble til Wybe", "Connect me to my Wybe colleague at https://&lt;slug&gt;.wybe.me", "connect to Wybe", "wybe connect", or any request to pair, link or hook up Claude Code with a Wybe node URL. ([wybe-labs/wybe-connect](https://github.com/wybe-labs/wybe-connect/tree/HEAD/packages/claude-code/skills/connect))
- **connect**: Connect a Telegram bot to THIS Claude Code session — pick from the configured bots, inbound Telegram messages for that bot start arriving here, and whichever session held it releases it. Use when the user asks to connect/attach/switch Telegram to this session. ([xlitedev/claude-telegram-switch](https://github.com/xlitedev/claude-telegram-switch/tree/HEAD/tg/skills/connect))
- **connect**: Use when the user wants to connect, bind, attach, or set up an xmemory instance for the current project — "connect xmemory", "which instances do I have", "bind my team knowledge instance here", "set up xmemory in this repo", "stop loading that instance here", or asks what xmemory knows about this working directory. Discovers the user's instances through the CLI, or an admin MCP entry when one is registered, and records the choice in a local .xmemory.json binding. ([xmemory-ai/claude-code-plugin](https://github.com/xmemory-ai/claude-code-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/connect))
- **connect**: Connect, reconnect or troubleshoot a dashboard MCP server so its tools show up for the assay plugin. Use when the tool list has no describe_dashboard tool, the user says the dashboard or its tools disappeared, asks how to connect a dashboard or where its MCP address comes from, got a 401 / needs to re-authorize, or is setting up a new machine. Read-only: it diagnoses, then walks the user through the connectors UI or prints the mcp add / login commands for them to run. Not for questions about the numbers on a dashboard that is already visible — that is assay:ask. 连接或修复数据看板（只做只读诊断，步骤和命令交给用户自己做）… ([xmindltd/assay](https://github.com/xmindltd/assay/tree/HEAD/plugins/assay/skills/connect))
- **connect**: 连接 WorkHub、定位具体工程任务并读取结构化上下文。用户首次使用 WorkHub，或要求切换任务、检查连接、读取任务背景时使用。 ([xujuntwt95329/workhub](https://github.com/xujuntwt95329/workhub/tree/HEAD/plugins/claude/workhub/skills/connect))
- **connect**: Pair this device with your Process OS account ([yoda-digital/p-os](https://github.com/yoda-digital/p-os/tree/HEAD/plugin/claude-code/skills/connect))
- **connect**: connect：把本仓库内嵌的 AI skills（prd-review、prd-regeneration）以符号链接安装到本机检测到的所有 AI Agent（Claude Code、Codex、Qwen Code、Grok 等），支持安装、列出已支持 agent、卸载、--path 指定目录。当用户需要将 prd-tech-review 能力同步到本地 agent 时使用。 ([zhiquanchi/prd-tech-review](https://github.com/zhiquanchi/prd-tech-review/tree/HEAD/connect))
